PETRI v6 SIMULATION: WHAT THE LAUNCHED RULES DO (sim/out-v6, run from sim/: `cargo run --release -- out-v6 all --seeds 8`, 81 s on 12 threads)

Both rule files were loaded exactly (contract/launch-rules.json for testnet, contract/launch-rules-mainnet.json for mainnet). Their economic
parameters are identical (checked field by field); they differ only in the house, keeper and wNEAR account names. Every run below uses
5% tides every 240 generations, tides skipped when no outside colony earned, credit payouts to colonies that never attached NEAR, the
house rules (never fed, no share, no sale, no PETRI to players, no bounty, no keeper pot), per-sponsor grants, slide licences (25% off,
10% royalty) and the v6 hot/cold books, all together. "Cash" = NEAR a player actually got back minus NEAR it actually paid (storage and
gas included). Players re-spend what they win until their budget is gone, so a kind that ends near minus its whole budget kept playing
to the last yoctoNEAR; the median and the (min..max) over seeds are shown.

THE CASH TABLE, IN PLAIN WORDS (young mainnet dish: the 5 house strains plus a few outsiders; section 1 has every number)

  Outsider type          alone, 7 d        alone, 14 d       with 5 outsiders, 7 d   with 20 outsiders, 7 d
  Penny (0.01 NEAR join, one block kept alive)
                         +0.44 NEAR        +0.37             -0.05 (all of its 0.05)  -0.05
  Methuselah (R-pentomino an hour, 0.5 budget)
                         +0.22             +0.06             +0.07 (-0.13..+0.44)     +0.27 (-0.07..+0.39)
  Builder (0.25 budget)  -0.25             -0.25             -0.25                    -0.25
  Raider / Defender      -                 -                 -0.25 / -0.25            -0.25 / -0.23
  Sniper                 -0.25             -0.25             -                        -0.16
  Whale (3 NEAR budget)  -3.00             -3.00             -                        -3.00
  Locker (2 NEAR)        -2.00             -2.00
  Self-sponsor (10 subs) -0.72             -0.72
  Racer                  -0.01             -0.01

  1. The first outsider DOES collect every tide - but only while it is alone. Alone, it took 100% of every one of the ~164 tides that
     ran in 7 days; a Penny that joined with 0.01 NEAR and kept one block alive netted +0.44 NEAR (+0.38..+0.51) of the house's NEAR.
     With 5 outsiders the first one's share of all tide shares fell to 21% (3%..78%) and it took 90%+ of a tide in only 6% of tides;
     with 20 outsiders, 1% and 0%. So "the first outsider collects nearly every tide" holds for the first hours or days of a dish
     with one player, not for a dish with a handful.
  2. On a young dish storage eats the tides: each tide that runs stores a 289-byte entry (0.00289 NEAR) paid out of its 5% share.
     With the vault the house fills, that was 39% of all tide pay with one outsider, 31% with 5 and 8% with 20.
  3. The mainnet house runs dry in about 5-6 days at 0.55 NEAR per strain (agents' v5 funding; the strains stop buying below
     0.12 NEAR). At 1/8 pace with the 100 PETRI/day cap the five strains spent 2.3 NEAR in that time (~0.08 NEAR per strain per day).
     After that nobody but the outsiders feeds the vault: in the 14-day columns nothing changes after week 1 for most kinds.
  4. Most kinds still lose. On the old societies with mainnet rules (section 2) builders, raiders, defenders, late joiners lose
     0.10-0.25, whales 1.3-3.0, the locker 0.41-1.71 (it no longer wins at 1,500 players: v5 had +0.59), self-sponsors 0.57-0.65,
     Penny 0.04. The methuselah is the one kind ahead at 15 and 150 players (+0.42, +0.41; v5 had +0.02, +0.14), -0.05 at 1,500 and
     -0.30 over 30 days. Storage takes 35-42% of all NEAR that comes in (70% on a dish with one outsider).
  5. "Free play never yields NEAR": a guest's own account never receives NEAR (all its shares, sales and bounties became credit;
     nothing is stranded). But free guests can pay PETRI royalties to a wallet that holds a slide, and the wallet sells them: about
     56 PETRI a week per operator, ~0.006 NEAR, against 0.53 NEAR of relayer money for its 10 guests. A leak, tiny (fixes.txt F4).
  6. Slide-royalty farming works: one account that mints slides of 8 common patterns at launch was paid 6,296 PETRI in a week by
     7,852 licensed plantings and netted +0.29 NEAR (+0.02..+0.36) in the 150-player society, +0.21 on the young dish; with royalties
     at 0 it nets -0.20. The other players lose 0.01-0.12 NEAR more when they plant under its licences than when no slides exist.
  7. Gas at scale contradicts "no call costs more as more colonies join". A join on a current dish is flat (3.7 TGas). But every
     call that loads the hot table pays for every colony with stake, and stake lingers ~60 tides (2.5 days) after a colony's last
     planting: on an active 30,000-colony dish 17,100 rows on average and 27,500 at most were hot. Under NEAR's protocol fees the
     largest plant was 237 TGas, buy 234, claim 233, a join that ran a catch-up 222; 16,102 calls (mostly plantings, ~3.9% of
     413,134) went over the 200 TGas the site prepays. Above ~21,000 hot rows a 200 TGas call can step only one generation, because
     the catch-up reserves gas to write both parts of the hot table. The contract's unit-runtime figures (plant 118.8, tide 148.2 at
     30,000 playing) charge ~39 Mgas per rewritten byte; the protocol charges ~71 (value + evicted value + memory), hence the gap.
     At the realistic 1,500 playing: plant ~15 TGas, advance through a paying tide ~46 (30 of it the payout reservation).
  8. The keeper pot never reaches anyone on mainnet unless an outsider calls `advance`: 1,480 PETRI sat in it after a week on the
     young 20-player dish. On testnet, agents/run.mjs (if the keeper is registered) advances whenever the pot holds over 50 PETRI,
     but v6 never pays the keeper the pot, so it advanced 4,820 times a day (1.7 NEAR/day at mainnet gas price, ~17 testnet NEAR/day).
  9. House-transfer laundering is impossible: every path (ft_transfer/ft_transfer_call to a non-house account, selling, planting a
     player's slide, the keeper pot, tide bounties and shares, council pacts) is refused by the v6 code (section 6, f6), and across
     all 343 runs the house moved 0 PETRI to non-house accounts. One unrestricted side door: a house slide NFT can be transferred.
 10. Board saturation (section 4): free (non-siege) cells fall from 71% with 5 players to 18% at 150, 8% at 1,500, 1.2% at 5,000 and
     0.8% at 15,000; free 2x2 spots from 63% to 0.2%. The cheapest legal block stays 4.2-5.6 mNEAR (the 333-byte deposit dominates;
     the PETRI price falls as counted cells rise), a siege block 5.2-9.4 mNEAR. Ground is never closed, only dearer.
 11. 90 days (section 3): a fixed 150-player society burns 5.5% of the agar by day 30 and then the dish stops (budgets gone, vault 0,
     tides stop). With 10 new outsiders a day the agar falls from 1,000,000 to 676,000 PETRI by day 90 (285,000 burned), feed per
     counted cell drifts from 0.0027 to 0.0017 PETRI per generation, a cell costs 0.83 PETRI, and a newcomer's block ~4.7 mNEAR
     throughout.

Rules that still look broken and the change that fixes each, with its measured effect: fixes.txt.

Petri v6 economy simulation (sim/src: main.rs, world.rs, model.rs) on the launched rules of both dishes.
All amounts NEAR unless marked. 'Cash' = NEAR actually received (tide claims, sales, bounties, sponsorship taken back) minus NEAR actually paid
(attached deposits with their storage, NEAR spent sponsoring, gas at 0.0001 NEAR/TGas). Each cell: median over seeds of the kind's mean player, (min..max).
Assumptions and the storage/gas model are in section 7. The house is modelled as agents/ plays it on mainnet (section 7).

  (f6) House-transfer laundering (house PETRI or NEAR reaching a player other than through the agar and the vault) — every path, as the v6 code has it:
    ft_transfer / ft_transfer_call house -> player      refused: token.rs:231 require!(!is_house(from) || is_house(to)); keepers are house (lib.rs is_house)
    house sells PETRI back for vault NEAR              refused: lib.rs sell() require!(!colony.house)
    house plants a player's slide (royalty to player)  refused: lib.rs plant() require!(!colony.house) when the holder is not the planter
    house or keeper takes the keeper pot               refused: lib.rs advance() !is_house(caller)
    tide bounty to a house runner (colony or keeper)   none: catch_up_by house_runner -> bounty 0, stays in the vault
    tide share / feed to house colonies                none: feed_with and tide_split_sparse skip `house` ids
    council pact house -> house, then house -> player  the second hop is the ft_transfer refusal above
    house slide NFT given to a player                  allowed (nft_transfer is not restricted), but the house never plants it again and its 16x16
                                                       fossil patterns are not what anyone plants: no royalty flow. The agents never transfer slides.
    house NEAR -> vault -> tides                       by design: the house's NEAR is the players' subsidy (section 1 measures who gets it)
    In every simulated run (the sim mirrors these checks; a house licence plant asserts): house PETRI moved to non-house accounts = 0 PETRI (max over 377 runs).

=== 7. Rules, storage and gas model, house model ===
Rules: ../contract/launch-rules.json (testnet) and ../contract/launch-rules-mainnet.json (mainnet), read with serde (unknown fields refused).
  Economic parameters identical in both files: YES (they differ only in house, keepers and wnear).
  128x128 dish, 15000 ms generations (5760 a day); feed_ppm 3, keeper_bps 200, reach 8, stake_keep_bps 8750, burn_bps 5000, payback 480, min_cell_price 0.5 PETRI, live_floor 1000,
  petri_price 0.0002 NEAR, max_supply 1000000 PETRI, min_join 0.01 NEAR, cell_fee 0.0001 NEAR, tide_every 240, tide_bps 500 (5% of the vault), tide_bounty_bps 100 (max 0.001 NEAR),
  sell_bps 5000, sell_cap_bps 1000, siege_x 3, buffer 3, max_plant_cells 64, max_catchup 120, max_lag 480, slide_cells_price 1 PETRI, slide_min_cells 5,
  slide_discount_bps 2500, slide_royalty_bps 1000, house 5 strains, keepers ["keeper.petri-dish.near"].
Storage, from the v6 borsh layout (key + value + 40 B per record; 1e19 yocto a byte):
  join 701 B for a 16-char wallet (Colony 251 + colony_of 63 + name 57 + balance 77 + Join entry 56 + cold slot 57 + 140 charged for the Buy entry,
    which is 105 B, 121 with `spent`); buy 140 B charged; plant_deposit = (240 + 77 [first hot row] + 82 [empty table] + 4c + note+64 + licence 33+L) B + 0.0001/cell;
  stored by a planting: Plant entry 111+2c (+16 spent, +49 licence with a 16-char holder) + index 53 + board 2c + 8 first time + note 49+n + hot row 77;
    the rest of the deposit (fee included) goes to the vault or credit pool; a row leaving the hot table frees 77 B to the contract.
  sell entry 105 B from the proceeds (a NEAR sale must also cover 239 B for a possible refund); claim entry 104 B from the NEAR (claims need > 343 B = 0.00343 NEAR owed);
  tide entry 236 + tide index 53 = 289 B = 0.00289 NEAR from the vault per tide that runs; skip entry 62 B (contract);
  sponsor (first grant) 471 B for a guest (entry 78+La+Ls, grants record 77+La+Ls, Unsponsor reserve 210); slide 4x4 486 B, 16x16 517 B (record, owner list, entry, 184 reserve).
  The contract's own code: 570994 B of wasm = 5.71 NEAR staked by the dish account.
Gas (NEAR protocol fee schedule; 1 TGas = 0.0001 NEAR at the minimum gas price): call 2.5 TGas + 60 Ggas per storage op + 12.2 Mgas per byte read / 70.7 Mgas
  per byte rewritten (value + evicted value + memory/registers) + 0.82 Mgas per wasm op. Hot table: loaded once per call that needs a colony's books (77 B a row
  read, ~600 ops a row to parse), the feed part (26 B) rewritten by every catch-up, the purse part (51 B) by tides and purse changes (~150 / 250 ops a row to write);
  a generation ~20k + 60 x live + 150 x colonies alive ops; a tide 4 writes + its 289 B + 60 ops a row + 400 per winner. A NEAR payout burns ~3.5 TGas and reserves
  30 TGas of the call's prepaid gas (counted in 'max gas'). Catch-up limit min(180, prepaid - 40 - rows x 77 B x 64 Mgas) TGas, as the contract.
  The contract's unit-runtime measurements (CHANGES-v6, host calls only) charge ~39 Mgas a rewritten byte, not ~71, so they read lower than this model.
The house (agents/run.mjs, strategies.mjs, council.mjs), approximated:
  mainnet: 5 strains attach their own NEAR (0.55 NEAR each, kept above 0.12 for gas: they stop buying there), join with 0.05, buy 0.02 when they cannot afford
  12 cells, act every [40..100] x 8 generations (temperament ranges), at most 60% of their PETRI and 100 PETRI a day per planting budget (10 always allowed),
  lab notes, never a siege, never a licence; candidates per temperament (gardener: blocks/beehives/loaves, eater walls, pulsars; raider: gliders, glider guns, LWSS;
  engineer: eaters, gliders, guns; wanderer: outposts in the emptiest agar, acorns, pulsars; chaos: blinkers, toads, acorn/R-pentomino). The model's pick is
  approximated as an ambitious candidate with probability 1/3 when affordable, else the first ordinary one. Every 12 h a strain fossilizes its densest 16x16
  window (>= 12 cells). Council pacts move <= 3 PETRI house-to-house and change nothing outside the house: not simulated. The keeper (a house account) advances
  only once an outsider has joined and the dish is >= 400 generations behind; its tides and the strains' tides pay no bounty (it stays in the vault).
  testnet: strains pay with 1.5 NEAR of sponsored credit each at full pace; the 3-wallet bench plays by the written rules; the keeper (registered) advances
  at 20 behind or whenever the pot holds over 50 PETRI; relayer guests (0.05 NEAR credit each).
Players: winnings, sales and bounties go back into the wallet; claims once a day; after each tide sellers race for the allowance (bots first, then a random
  quarter of the rest selling above 16 cells' price). Every non-house player plants under a slide's licence when one exists for its pattern (25% off, a 10%
  royalty to the holder), in the slide's orientation. Kinds: see sim/src/world.rs `Kind` (Penny = min_join 0.01 NEAR, one block kept alive and paid).

=== 8. Per-scenario detail ===
  Columns per player (median over seeds of the kind's mean player): attached / sponsored / gas paid; claimed / sold / bounty / reclaimed received; cash and
  paper; credit won and left; royalties received and paid (PETRI); e/s = PETRI earned / spent; feed% and tide% = the kind's share of the feed and of tide shares.
